# قانون التجارة (`commerce`): every article that is not identical to the official consolidated text

Draft, for lawyer review. Part of [the corpus verification of 2026-11-12](../2026-11-12-corpus-vs-official.md). The corpus side is the text as the product parses it (the corpus as it is); the official side is the Legislation and Opinion Bureau's consolidated text (LOB id 1028). The full texts of every article, identical ones included, are in [`2026-11-12-corpus-diff.jsonl`](../2026-11-12-corpus-diff.jsonl). Word differences are shown after the normalisation of level (b) and without the heading lines and paragraph numbers, so each line names a real change of words.

> **Review of 2026-11-13.** The first fix attempt was reverted; nothing below has been applied. The class "amendment not applied" is the detector's output, which the review found overstated: it fired on spelling variants, headings and quoted-for-deletion words, and it treated LOB as the ceiling (main report, [section 11](../2026-11-12-corpus-vs-official.md#11-known-defects-of-the-first-fix-attempt-reverted), D1 and D2). For this law: LOB's amending-law list is empty (its SHA-256 is that of `[]`), and no corpus article equals any LOB version. The amendment rule could not fire, so this is an empty result, not a clean bill (D5).
